Abstract: Railway track intrusions pose a critical safety challenge for Indian Railways, encompassing wildlife incursions and deliberate malicious obstructions. The December 2025 collision in Assam, in which seven elephants were killed by the Rajdhani Express, underscores the urgency of effective real-time detection. Existing solutions such as the optical fiber-based Gajraj system suffer from prohibitive costs (\$1000/km) and high false alarm rates, limiting deployment to only 20 of India's 101 elephant corridors. This paper proposes NETRA, a cost-effective, internet-independent intrusion detection system deployed on Raspberry Pi Zero W and Raspberry Pi 4 edge platforms. NETRA employs probabilistic sensor fusion integrating a PIR motion sensor and an HC-SR04 ultrasonic distance sensor with a tunable threshold (tau_c = 0.65), enabling event-driven camera activation that reduces unnecessary visual processing by 52%. Upon confirmed intrusion, edge-AI classification using MobileNet-SSD (Pi Zero) or YOLOv5 ONNX (Pi 4) identifies threats including humans, large animals, and track obstructions. Confirmed threats are transmitted via LoRa (868 MHz) to alert the locomotive driver within 2.4 seconds end-to-end. Experimental evaluation across 113 motion events demonstrated 95% detection accuracy with zero false alarms through probabilistic fusion, compared to 85% for binary methods. Raspberry Pi 4 with YOLOv5 achieved 83.5% elephant F1-score, a 5.6x improvement over Pi Zero's heuristic approach (14.8%). LoRa communication achieved 100% packet delivery across 1-2 km in field trials. NETRA reduces deployment cost by 75% (\$247/km vs \$1000/km for Gajraj) while providing unified detection of both wildlife and obstruction threats.

</details>


### [25] [Dimensional Coactivation for Representational Consistency in Frozen Vision Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08249)
*Izaldein Al-Zyoud Abdulmotaleb El Saddik*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出了Dimensional Coactivation (DCA)方法，用于测量冻结视觉基础模型内部表示的一致性，特别是检测同一图像中不同语义区域是否在特征空间中保持连贯性。该方法在深度伪造检测任务上表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冻结的视觉基础模型不仅提取特征，还通过学习的坐标系组织图像。作者想知道这个坐标系在单个输入内部是否保持内部一致性，从而引出表示一致性研究：冻结基础模型是否一致地表示一个样本的各个语义子区域。

Method: 提出了Dimensional Coactivation (DCA)方法，这是一种按维度测量的工具，通过比较语义区域来评估相同特征维度是否在这些区域间共同激活。与传统相似性度量不同，DCA有意避免中心化、L2归一化和全Gram耦合，因为这些操作在坐标系固定的内部样本设置中不适用，原始幅度携带信号信息。

Result: 使用冻结的DINOv3特征，DCA在深度伪造检测任务上取得卓越性能：眼睛-嘴巴-鼻子指纹在CelebDF-v2上达到0.9106 AUC，在DFD上达到0.9289 AUC（FF++ c23跨数据集迁移）。消融实验强烈验证设计：重新引入中心化使AUC降至0.459，L2归一化降至0.862，跨维度耦合降至0.478。用FaRL替换DINOv3使AUC降至0.582。

Conclusion: DCA依赖于稳定的每维度坐标系，而不仅仅是区域提取。这些结果将DCA定位为测量冻结基础模型中内部样本表示一致性的工具，深度伪造检测作为首个验证任务。该方法揭示了合成面部虽然能再现逼真的眼睛、鼻子和嘴巴，但破坏了真实面部中各区域之间的表示结构。

Abstract: Frozen vision foundation models do not merely extract features; they organize images through a learned coordinate system. We ask whether that coordinate system remains internally coherent within a single input. This leads to Representational Consistency: the study of whether a frozen foundation model represents one sample coherently across its semantic subregions. We introduce Dimensional Coactivation (DCA), a per-dimension instrument for measuring this coherence. DCA compares semantic regions by asking whether the same feature dimensions coactivate across them. Unlike classical similarity measures, it deliberately avoids centering, L2 normalization, and full Gram coupling. These operations are useful when comparing different models or distributions, but they are mismatched to the intra-sample setting, where the coordinate system is fixed and raw magnitude carries signal. Deepfake detection provides a natural validation task. Synthetic faces may reproduce plausible eyes, noses, and mouths while breaking the representational structure that links those regions in real faces. Using frozen DINOv3 features, DCA exposes this break: an eyes-mouth-nose fingerprint achieves 0.9106 AUC on CelebDF-v2 and 0.9289 on DFD under FF++ c23 cross-dataset transfer. The design is also sharply validated by ablation: reintroducing centering collapses CelebDF-v2 AUC to 0.459, L2 normalization reduces it to 0.862, and cross-dimension coupling reduces it to 0.478. Finally, replacing DINOv3 with FaRL collapses CelebDF-v2 AUC to 0.582. DCA therefore depends on a stable per-dimension coordinate system, not on region extraction alone. These results position DCA as an instrument for measuring intra-sample representational coherence in frozen foundation models, with deepfake detection as the first validation task.

</details>


### [26] [Why Do DiT Editors Drift? Plug-and-Play Low Frequency Alignment in VAE Latent Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08250)
*Xiaoce Wang,Sifan Zhou,Kaifei Wang,Leli Xu,Xuerui Qiu,Tao He,Ming Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VAE-LFA：一种无需训练、即插即用的方法，通过在VAE潜空间中进行低频对齐，有效抑制多轮编辑中的累积语义漂移，提高语义一致性和视觉保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散变换器（DiTs）在多轮图像编辑中面临渐进语义漂移和质量退化问题。研究者从潜空间频率角度分析该问题，发现DiT引入主导的低频漂移在多轮编辑中累积导致语义不对齐，而VAE提供相对稳定的重建偏差。

Method: 提出VAE-LFA方法：1）通过低通滤波器分解编辑轮次间的潜空间差异；2）将低频统计量与先前轮次的指数移动平均值对齐；3）对白盒模型，通过消除冗余VAE往返无缝集成到编辑流程；对黑盒模型，通过现成VAE执行轮次间潜空间对齐。

Result: 广泛实验表明，VAE-LFA在多样多轮编辑场景（包括受控图像和野生图像）中显著改善了语义一致性和视觉保真度，有效抑制了累积语义漂移，同时保留了高频细节。

Conclusion: VAE-LFA是一种训练免费、无需真实先验或扩散参数访问的有效方法，适用于白盒和黑盒DiT编辑器，为解决多轮图像编辑中的语义漂移问题提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Recent advances in diffusion transformers (DiTs) have enabled promising single-turn image editing capabilities. However, multi-turn editing often leads to progressive semantic drift and quality degradation.In this work, we study this problem from a latent-space frequency perspective by decomposing the editing process into two functional components: VAE and DiT. Through systematic analysis in the VAE latent space, we uncover that the DiT introduces dominant low-frequency drift that accumulates as semantic misalignment across editing rounds, while the VAE contributes comparatively stable reconstruction bias.Based on this insight, we propose VAE-LFA (Low Frequency Alignment), a training-free, plug-and-play method that performs alignment in VAE latent space. VAE-LFA decomposes latent discrepancies across editing rounds via low-pass filtering, and aligns low-frequency statistics to an exponential moving average of previous rounds, effectively suppressing accumulated semantic drift while preserving high-frequency details.Our method requires no retraining, ground-truth priors, or access to diffusion parameters, making it applicable to both white-box and black-box DiT editors. For white-box models, VAE-LFA is seamlessly integrated into the editing pipeline by eliminating redundant VAE round trips; for black-box models, it operates via an off-the-shelf VAE to perform inter-round latent alignment.Extensive experiments demonstrate that VAE-LFA improves semantic consistency and visual fidelity across diverse multi-turn editing scenarios, including both controlled and in-the-wild images.

</details>


### [27] [Multimodal Emotion Recognition via Causal-Diffusion Bridge (Affect-Diff)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08252)
*Ankit Sanjyal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Affect-Diff：一个因果-扩散桥接框架，通过因果图重加权、beta-VAE瓶颈和停止梯度的1D DDPM先验，解决了CMU-MOSEI数据集中的极端类别不平衡问题，在平衡准确率上相比基线提升18%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CMU-MOSEI数据集存在极端不平衡问题：Happy类别占65.9%，而三个Ekman类别合计不到7%，导致标准融合模型为最大化准确率而完全忽略少数情绪类别。

Method: 提出Affect-Diff框架，包含三个联合训练机制：1) NOTEARS学习的因果图，在融合前重新加权模态贡献；2) beta-VAE瓶颈用于正则化潜在压缩；3) 停止梯度的1D DDPM先验，防止潜在空间中的多数类坍塌。

Result: 在3,292个对齐的CMU-MOSEI样本上，Affect-Diff达到验证平衡准确率0.384，比最强基线TETFN（0.324）相对提升18%。所有基线在Fear、Disgust和Surprise类别上的F1均为零。消融实验显示扩散先验（无则下降24%）和因果图（无则下降13%）均有独立贡献。只有确定性编码器变体能检测所有六种情绪类别。

Conclusion: Affect-Diff通过因果-扩散桥接框架有效解决了多模态情绪识别中的极端类别不平衡问题，KL正则化强度被证明是少数类敏感度的直接调节杠杆。

Abstract: Multimodal emotion recognition on CMU-MOSEI faces an extreme imbalance as Happy accounts for 65.9% of samples while three Ekman categories collectively represent under 7%, causing standard fusion models to maximize accuracy by ignoring minority emotions entirely. We present Affect-Diff, a Causal-Diffusion Bridge that addresses this through three jointly trained mechanisms: a NOTEARS-learned causal graph that re-weights modality contributions before fusion, a beta-VAE bottleneck for regularized latent compression, and a stop-gradiented 1D DDPM prior that structures the latent space against majority-class collapse. On 3,292 aligned CMU-MOSEI samples, Affect-Diff achieves validation balanced accuracy 0.384, an 18% relative improvement over the strongest baseline (TETFN: 0.324), while all evaluated baselines produce zero F1 on Fear, Disgust, and Surprise. Ablation studies confirm independent, non-redundant contributions from the diffusion prior (-24% without it) and causal graph (-13%). Notably, only the deterministic-encoder variant detects all six emotion classes, revealing KL regularization strength as a direct lever for minority-class sensitivity.

</details>


### [28] [Is Class Signal Clustered or Routed in Task-Induced Implicit Neural Representation Weight Spaces?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08281)
*Xinyi Guo,Mingyi He,Haobin Ding,Weiming Chen,Xinrui Chen,Jiawen Li,Di Zhang,Minxi Ouyang,Yizhi Wang,Xitong Ling*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究发现隐式神经表示（INR）权重空间的几何聚类假设不成立，分类器不是继承而是构造类对齐的几何结构，通过特定低维路径进行读取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试隐式神经表示（INRs）中权重空间的几何假设：即通过分类器反馈，图像特定的权重应该在共享锚点坐标中按类别聚类，从而有利于图像分类。

Method: 在SIREN-based Meta Weight Transformer（MWT）体系中进行实验，使用端到端训练元学习共享初始化和内部循环更新计划来拟合图像特定SIRENs。采用token-flow诊断分析几何结构演化，识别低维因果读取路径，并进行针对性控制实验。

Result: 发现权重空间几何聚类假设失败：监督聚类压力与训练后读取器准确性不可靠地相关，聚类甚至可能在提高局部邻域类别一致性的同时降低读取器性能。分类器构造而非继承类对齐几何，类对齐邻域仅在读取器后期交互后才变得具有预测性。识别出增强权重token中的原生SIREN偏置列作为低维、样本依赖的因果读取路径。

Conclusion: 任务诱导的INR权重可分类不是因为它们形成原始几何聚类，而是因为它们的类别信号通过读取器进行路由。这激发了通过加强读取器路由、添加显式偏置路径或使用更密集内部循环拟合的干预措施，在某些训练约定下能超越共享锚点基线。

Abstract: Implicit neural representations (INRs) encode images as neural-network weights, making image classification a problem of weight-space classifiability. A natural geometric hypothesis is that classifier feedback should make image-specific weights cluster by class in the shared-anchor coordinate. We test this hypothesis in the SIREN-based Meta Weight Transformer (MWT) regime, where end-to-end training meta-learns a shared initialization and inner-loop update schedule for fitting image-specific SIRENs. We find that this prediction fails. Exposed weight-space geometry and supervised clustering pressure do not reliably track trained-reader accuracy; clustering can even make local neighborhoods more class-consistent while making the trained reader worse. Crucially, the reader constructs rather than inherits class-aligned geometry: token-flow diagnostics show that class-aligned neighborhoods become strongly predictive of trained-reader accuracy only after late reader interactions, not in the input coordinate. We further identify the native SIREN bias column in the augmented weight token as a low-dimensional, sample-dependent causal readout route for the trained reader; targeted controls rule out generic scalar-column and marginal-distribution artifacts. The diagnosis motivates interventions that strengthen reader routing, add an explicit bias route, or use denser inner-loop fitting; under the lane-specific training conventions used here, route-directed variants often outperform the shared-anchor baseline but interact non-additively. Task-induced INR weights are classifiable not because they form raw geometric clusters, but because their class signal is routed through the reader.

</details>


### [29] [Distill, Diffuse, and Semanticize (DDS): Annotation-Free 3D Scene Understanding Based on Multi-Granularity Distillation and Graph-Diffusion-Based Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08293)
*Yijing Wang,Ruonan Li,Qilin Wang,Rongqiang Zhao,Jie Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于多粒度蒸馏和图扩散分割的无标注3D场景语义理解方法，通过视觉知识引导和超点图扩散实现高效的全局语义传播，并通过分割-聚类关联进行语义推理，显著提升了无标注方法的性能指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D语义场景理解在多个领域有广泛应用，但密集点云标注成本极高，使得全监督3D语义学习难以扩展。现有的无标注方法存在对象级一致性弱、全局分组效率低、以及分割区域类别不可识别等问题。

Method: 采用多粒度蒸馏和图扩散分割方法：1) 利用结构化视觉知识引导和超点图扩散实现高效全局语义传播，缓解区域级语义不一致问题；2) 通过分割-聚类关联进行语义推理，为分割的3D区域分配可解释的类别名称。

Result: 在真实世界数据集上的广泛实验表明，与现有先进的无标注基线相比，该方法最多将oAcc、mAcc和mIoU分别提升了5.9%、8.1%和2.4%。

Conclusion: 该方法为可扩展的无标注3D场景理解提供了有前景的解决方案，特别适用于需要同时进行对象分割和语义识别的实际场景，证明了其在真实世界应用中的有效性。

Abstract: 3D semantic scene understanding has broad applications in digital twins, autonomous driving, smart agriculture, and embodied perception. However, dense point-wise annotation for point clouds is extremely expensive, making fully supervised 3D semantic learning difficult to scale. Recent annotation-free methods can discover semantic regions without manual 3D labels, but they often suffer from weak object-level consistency, inefficient global grouping, and category-agnostic segmented regions. We propose an annotation-free 3D scene semantic understanding method based on multi-granularity distillation and graph-diffusion-based segmentation. The proposed method first leverages structured visual knowledge guidance and superpoint graph diffusion to perform efficient global semantic propagation, alleviating the problem of inconsistent region-level semantics. It then conducts semantic inference through segmentation-cluster association, assigning interpretable category names to segmented 3D regions and improving the overall effectiveness of annotation-free 3D semantic understanding. Extensive experiments on real-world datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed framework. Compared with the advanced existing annotation-free baselines, our method improves oAcc, mAcc, and mIoU by 5.9%, 8.1%, and 2.4% at most, respectively. These results highlight the promise of the proposed framework for scalable annotation-free 3D scene understanding, especially in real-world scenarios requiring both object segmentation and semantic recognition.

</details>


### [30] [BenchHAR: Benchmarking Self-Supervised Learning for Generalizable Sensor-based Activity Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08296)
*Yize Cai,Rui Feng,Anlan Yu,Baoshen Guo,Zhiqing Hong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了BenchHAR框架，系统评估自监督学习方法在传感器人体活动识别中的泛化性能，发现现有方法泛化能力不足，并提出了改进见解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可穿戴传感器的人体活动识别在医疗和行为科学中有广泛应用，但数据异质性和标注数据稀缺限制了其实际应用的泛化能力。现有自监督学习方法在视觉和语言领域表现出色，但在传感器HAR领域的泛化性能缺乏系统比较。

Method: 提出了BenchHAR统一评估框架，包含大规模数据集（约258K样本），评估了8种代表性自监督学习方法在12种编码器-分类器架构上的泛化性能。重点关注模型在未见目标分布上的表现。

Result: 发现现有SSL方法泛化性能不佳，具体发现：（1）混合范式（重构+对比预训练）效果最佳，CNN编码器泛化能力最强，更具表现力的分类器架构可进一步提升泛化；（2）增加预训练数据量持续改善泛化，但增加标注数据收益有限，非下游类别的未标注数据无帮助；（3）商用设备数据比研究设备泛化更好，肢体数据向躯干转移更有效。

Conclusion: BenchHAR为可泛化的传感器HAR系统提供了统一基准和实用见解，揭示了现有方法的局限性，并指导了如何改进SSL方法以获得更好的跨域泛化能力。

Abstract: Human Activity Recognition (HAR) from wearable sensors supports broad healthcare and behavior science applications. However, data heterogeneity and the scarcity of labeled data limit its real-world generalization. Recent advances in self-supervised learning (SSL) in vision and language domains have shown strong capability for learning generalizable representations from unlabeled data. Yet, few studies have systematically compared the generalization performance of SSL methods or explored how to adapt them for generalizable HAR. To address these gaps, we present BenchHAR, a unified framework for evaluating the generalization capability of SSL methods for sensor-based HAR on unseen target distributions. BenchHAR curates a large-scale dataset (~258K samples) and evaluates eight representative SSL methods across 12 encoder-classifier architectures. Our results reveal that existing SSL methods struggle to achieve satisfactory generalization performance. We find that: (1) For HAR models, the hybrid paradigm (combining reconstruction and contrastive pretraining) achieves the best overall performance. The CNN encoder exhibits the strongest ability to learn generalizable representations, while more expressive classifier architectures further improve generalization. (2) For data scale, increasing the amount of pretraining data from downstream activity classes consistently improves generalization, while adding more labeled data yields limited gains. Interestingly, incorporating unlabeled data from non-downstream activity classes does not improve generalization. (3) Sensor data collected from custom-grade devices generalizes better than that from research-grade devices, and data from limb transfers more effectively to trunk positions. BenchHAR provides a unified benchmark and actionable insights for generalizable sensor-based HAR systems. Our code is available at https://github.com/saiketa/HAR-Bench.

</details>


### [31] [An Efficient Token Compression Framework for Visual Object Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08329)
*Weijing Wu,Qihua Liang,Bineng Zhong,Haiying Xia,Zhiyi Mo,Shuxiang Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ETCTrack是一种压缩-交互的视觉跟踪框架，通过自适应令牌压缩器动态构建紧凑模板令牌，减少特征冗余，在降低计算成本的同时保持跟踪精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Transformer-based跟踪器使用大量历史模板帧来获取时空线索，但这导致输入视觉令牌数量庞大，造成两个问题：1) 二次计算负担；2) 可能降低跟踪器整体性能。需要消除特征级冗余以同时优化性能和计算成本。

Method: 提出压缩-交互跟踪框架ETCTrack：1) 自适应令牌压缩器(Adaptive Token Compressor)动态过滤冗余视觉令牌，构建紧凑且具有高度判别性的模板令牌；2) 分层交互编码器(Hierarchical Interaction Encoder)处理精炼的模板令牌，与搜索特征进行深度自适应交互，确保精确目标定位。

Result: 在7个基准测试中超越当前最先进跟踪器。ETCTrack-B224版本将模板令牌数量减少60%，MACs降低21.4%，精度仅下降0.4%。

Conclusion: ETCTrack通过学习压缩历史模板令牌构建鲁棒目标表示的方法，有效解决了大规模模板令牌带来的计算负担和性能下降问题，实现了性能与计算效率的良好平衡。

Abstract: Refining visual representations by eliminating their internal feature-level redundancy is crucial for simultaneously optimizing the performance and computational cost of models in visual tracking. To enhance their performance, many contemporary Transformer-based trackers leverage a larger number of historical template frames to capture richer spatio-temporal cues. However, this strategy leads to a massive number of input visual tokens. This creates two critical issues: it imposes a quadratic computational burden and can also degrade the tracker's overall performance. To bridge this gap, we propose a compress-then-interact tracking framework, ETCTrack, that learns to efficiently compress template tokens from historical template frames into a robust target representation, moving beyond handcrafted rules. Our method first employs the Adaptive Token Compressor to dynamically construct compact yet highly discriminative template tokens by filtering out redundant visual tokens. These refined template tokens are then processed by our Hierarchical Interaction Encoder to achieve a deep, adaptive interaction with the search features. Refined search features ensure subsequent precise target localization. Experiments on seven benchmarks demonstrate that our method outperforms current state-of-the-art trackers. ETCTrack-B224 reduces the number of template tokens by 60%, leading to a 21.4% reduction in MACs with only a 0.4% drop in accuracy. The source code are available at https://github.com/PJD-WJ/ETCTrack.

</details>


### [32] [PaceVGGT: Pre-Alternating-Attention Token Pruning for Visual Geometry Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08371)
*Haotang Li,Zhenyu Qi,Shaohan Henry Wang,Kebin Peng,Zi Wang,Qing Guo,Sen He,Huanrui Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PaceVGGT是一种用于VGGT模型的预注意层令牌剪枝框架，通过训练轻量级令牌评分器在交替注意力层前剪枝DINO补丁令牌，在保持重建质量的同时显著降低推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VGGT模型中的交替注意力(AA)堆栈在总令牌数量上具有二次缩放性，使得长视频片段计算成本高昂。现有的令牌缩减加速器在AA内部操作，无法压缩进入AA的补丁网格，因此需要更高效的加速方法。

Method: 1. 训练轻量级令牌评分器从DINO特征估计每个令牌的重要性
2. 首先针对未剪枝骨干中的AA内部注意力目标进行蒸馏训练
3. 在下游相机、深度和点云地图损失下进行精炼
4. 使用每帧保留预算固定骨干可见序列长度
5. 采用重要性自适应合并/剪枝分配，在高显著性帧下保留残差内容
6. 添加特征引导恢复模块重建预测头所需的密集空间网格

Result: 1. 在ScanNet-50和7-Scenes数据集上，PaceVGGT保持在重建质量-延迟前沿的同时减少推理延迟
2. 在ScanNet-50上，相对于未修改的VGGT(N=300)延迟减少5.1倍
3. 相对于LiteVGGT(N=1000)延迟减少1.47倍

Conclusion: 预注意层剪枝是冻结VGGT风格几何变换器的一种可行的加速途径，能够在保持重建质量的同时显著提高推理效率。

Abstract: Visual Geometry Transformer (VGGT) is a strong feed-forward model for multiple 3D tasks, but its Alternating-Attention (AA) stack scales quadratically in the total token count, making long clips expensive.
  Existing token-reduction accelerators operate inside AA, leaving the patch grid that enters AA uncompressed.
  We introduce PaceVGGT, a pre-AA token pruning framework that prunes DINO patch tokens before the first AA block of a frozen VGGT. PaceVGGT trains a lightweight Token Scorer that estimates per-token importance from DINO features. The scorer is first distilled against an AA-internal attention target from the unpruned backbone, then refined under downstream camera, depth, and point-map losses.
  A per-frame keep budget fixes the backbone-visible sequence length, while an importance-adaptive merge/prune assignment preserves residual content from high-saliency frames under a fixed total merge budget.
  A Feature-guided Restoration module reconstructs the dense spatial grid required by the prediction heads.
  On ScanNet-50 and 7-Scenes, PaceVGGT remains on the reconstruction quality--latency frontier while reducing inference latency.
  On ScanNet-50, it reduces latency by \(5.1\times\) over unmodified VGGT at \(N=300\) and \(1.47\times\) over LiteVGGT at \(N=1000\).
  These results identify pre-AA pruning as a viable acceleration route for frozen VGGT-style geometry transformers.

</details>


### [33] [Decoupling Endpoint and Semantic Transition Learning for Zero-Shot Composed Image Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08389)
*Mingyu Liu,Sihan Huang,Yijia Fan,Yinlin Yan,Quan Zhang,Jian-Fang Hu,Jianhuang Lai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DeCIR是一种用于零样本组合图像检索的投影基方法，通过解耦端点匹配和语义转换学习，使用低秩方向合并技术，在不增加推理复杂度的情况下显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统投影基ZS-CIR方法在复杂语义修改任务上表现不佳，存在语义转换瓶颈问题。端点级匹配可能导致编辑文本仅作为目标侧属性提示而非源条件语义转换。同时，向同一文本适配器添加语义转换监督会造成端点与转换之间的学习冲突。

Method: DeCIR解耦端点和转换学习：1)从图像-标题对构建配对的前向/反向编辑元组；2)为端点对齐和语义转换对齐训练独立的低秩文本适配器分支；3)通过低秩方向合并技术将两个分支合并为一个可部署适配器。

Result: 在CIRR、CIRCO、FashionIQ和GeneCIS等多个基准数据集上的广泛实验表明，DeCIR能够一致地改进投影基ZS-CIR性能，且不增加推理复杂度。

Conclusion: 通过解耦端点匹配和语义转换学习，DeCIR成功解决了投影基ZS-CIR中的语义转换瓶颈和端点-转换冲突问题，为轻量级组合图像检索提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Zero-shot composed image retrieval (ZS-CIR) retrieves a target image from a reference image and a text modification without human-annotated CIR triplets. Projection-based ZS-CIR methods are attractive because they do not rely on LLMs at inference and remain lightweight, but they often underperform LLM-based approaches on complex semantic modifications. This gap reflects a semantic transition bottleneck in projection-based ZS-CIR: endpoint-level matching can let the edit text act as a target-side attribute cue rather than grounding it as a source-conditioned semantic transition. We further show that adding semantic transition supervision to the same text adapter creates an endpoint--transition conflict between endpoint alignment and semantic transition alignment. To address this conflict, DeCIR decouples endpoint and transition learning. It constructs paired forward/reverse edit tuples from image-caption pairs, trains separate low-rank text adapter branches for endpoint alignment and semantic transition alignment, and merges them with Low-Rank Directional Merge (LRDM) into one deployable adapter. Extensive experiments on CIRR, CIRCO, FashionIQ, and GeneCIS demonstrate that DeCIR consistently improves projection-based ZS-CIR without increasing inference complexity.

</details>


### [34] [Delivering Science as a Service: Sci-Orchestra's Cloud-Native Approach to HPC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08396)
*Harinarayan Krishnan,Shubhabrata Mukerjee,Jeffrey Donatelli,Daniela Ushizima*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Sci-Orchestra是一个分层编排框架，旨在完全自动化实验工作流程，使科学家能够专注于科学发现而非基础设施管理，通过API驱动接口提供安全认证、资源管理和跨异构HPC环境的可扩展部署，特别包含促进跨机构协作的自主市场。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代计算环境的复杂性日益增加，研究人员常常被基础设施管理、认证协议和容器部署等后端操作所困扰，分散了他们对科学发现的专注力。

Method: 采用分层编排框架设计，通过API驱动接口抽象执行过程，基于Kubernetes架构在不同高性能计算环境中实现安全认证、资源管理和可扩展部署。创新性地引入自主市场机制，提供直观用户界面，使研究人员能够通过简单选择快速部署和共享专业服务。

Result: 该系统消除了复杂安装和技术设置的需求，提供了安全的执行环境，支持外部合作者在无需源代码交换的情况下测试和验证专有工具，实现了"黑盒"式互操作性，保护知识产权的同时促进无缝集成到更广泛的科学流程中。

Conclusion: Sci-Orchestra通过自动化工作流程和促进跨机构协作，加速了从实验室原型到工业规模应用的过渡，使研究人员能够更专注于科学发现本身。

Abstract: The increasing complexity of modern computational environments often burdens researchers with infrastructure management, authentication protocols, and container deployments. We present Sci-Orchestra, a layered orchestration framework designed to fully automate experimental workflows, allowing scientists to prioritize scientific discovery over backend operations. By abstracting execution through an API-driven interface, the system assumes responsibility for secure authentication, resource management, and scalable deployment across diverse high-performance computing environments using Kubernetes architectures. A key innovation of Sci-Orchestra is its autonomous marketplace, which serves as a catalyst for cross-institutional collaboration. Through an intuitive user interface, researchers can rapidly deploy and share specialized services via simple selections, eliminating the need for complex installations and technical setups. This modular infrastructure is specifically designed to facilitate industry partnerships as it provides a secure execution environment and allows external collaborators to test and validate proprietary tools without the need for source-code exchange. This ``black-box'' interoperability protects intellectual property while enabling seamless integration into broader scientific pipelines, ultimately accelerating the transition from laboratory prototypes to industrial-scale applications.

</details>


### [35] [SYNCR: A Cross-Video Reasoning Benchmark with Synthetic Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08412)
*Sara Ghazanfari,Siddharth Garg,Prashanth Krishnamurthy,Farshad Khorrami*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SYNCR是一个用于多视频推理的合成基准测试，通过程序化验证的基准数据评估多模态大语言模型在跨视频推理方面的能力，揭示当前模型与人类表现之间的显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型在单视频理解方面进展迅速，但在跨多个独立视频流的推理能力方面仍未被充分理解。现有的多视频基准测试主要依赖人类标注的真实世界素材，限制了空间、时间和物理基础事实的精确性，难以诊断模型失败的原因。

Method: 使用Habitat、Kubric和CLEVRER模拟器引擎构建SYNCR合成基准测试，包含8,163个多视频问答对，基于9,650个独特视频。评估涵盖四个诊断支柱的八个任务：时序对齐、空间跟踪、比较推理和整体综合。

Result: 领先的开源和闭源MLLM的零样本评估显示：最佳模型平均准确率仅为52.5%，而人类基线为89.5%。模型在时序排序上表现相对较好，但在精确的物理和空间推理上困难重重，最佳模型在运动学比较任务上仅达到26.0%准确率。参数扩展和推理专业化后训练能改进时序对齐能力，但不能可靠解决细粒度物理跟踪或全局空间综合问题。

Conclusion: SYNCR基准测试揭示了当前MLLM在跨视频推理方面的严重不足，特别是在物理和空间推理方面。该基准能够跟踪模型在真实世界多视频基准测试中的趋势，同时暴露出现有评估未能充分体现的推理能力。需要进一步研究来改进模型在细粒度物理理解和空间综合方面的能力。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have made rapid progress in single-video understanding, yet their ability to reason across multiple independent video streams remains poorly understood. Existing multi-video benchmarks rely largely on human-annotated real-world footage, limiting the precision of spatial, temporal, and physical ground truth and making it difficult to diagnose model failures. We introduce SYNCR, a controlled synthetic benchmark for cross-video reasoning with programmatically verified grounding. Built using Habitat, Kubric, and CLEVRER simulator engines, SYNCR contains 8,163 multi-video question-answer pairs grounded in 9,650 unique videos. It evaluates MLLMs across eight tasks spanning four diagnostic pillars: Temporal Alignment, Spatial Tracking, Comparative Reasoning, and Holistic Synthesis.
  Our zero-shot evaluation of leading open- and closed-weight MLLMs reveals a substantial gap between current models and humans: the best model achieves only 52.5% average accuracy, compared to an 89.5% human baseline. Models perform relatively well on temporal ordering but struggle with precise physical and spatial reasoning, with the best model reaching only 26.0% accuracy on Kinematic Comparison. We further find that parameter scaling and reasoning-specialized post-training improve temporal alignment capabilities, but do not reliably address fine-grained physical tracking or global spatial synthesis. Finally, an exploratory sim-to-real correlation analysis suggests that several SYNCR tasks track model-level trends on real-world multi-video benchmarks, while also exposing reasoning capabilities underrepresented by existing evaluations. Code available at https://github.com/SaraGhazanfari/SYNCR.

</details>


### [36] [Beyond Bag-of-Patches: Learning Global Layout via Textual Supervision for Late-Interaction Visual Document Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08421)
*Pascal Tilli,Mohsen Mesgar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种增强视觉文档检索模型的方法，通过在现有架构基础上添加可学习的全局布局嵌入，而不改变推理过程，从而显著提升检索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的视觉文档检索（VDR）模型主要依赖局部交互架构，这些架构使用局部补丁嵌入来匹配查询标记。虽然效率高，但这种方法过于强调局部相似性而忽略了文档的全局布局结构。在实际应用中，文档相关性往往源自文档的整体布局结构（包含图像、表格和文本的异构布局），这导致现有方法产生错误。

Method: 提出了一种多模态编码器，在不改变推理过程的前提下，通过添加全局布局嵌入来增强局部补丁表示。该模型通过编码包含文档布局信息的文本描述进行训练，使文档布局变得可学习。

Result: 在四个ViDoRe-v2数据集上的实验表明，该方法相比最强架构可比的基线模型（ColPali/ColQwen），在nDCG@5指标上提升了+2.4，在MAP@5指标上提升了+2.3。对ColQwen基线在单个数据集上都取得了统计显著的性能增益。

Conclusion: 通过引入可学习的全局布局表示，论文成功解决了传统视觉文档检索模型中局部相似性优先于全局布局结构的问题。这种方法在不改变现有推理架构的前提下，显著提升了检索性能，证明了文档布局信息对于准确估计文档相关性的重要性。

Abstract: Visual Document Retrieval (VDR) models mostly rely on late interaction architectures, in which documents are represented by a set of local patch embeddings and then matched against query tokens. While efficient, this architecture prioritizes local similarity over global layout structure of documents to estimate relevancy between documents and query. In practice, this leads to errors as relevance originates from layout structure of documents with heterogeneous layouts combining figures, tables, and text. We make document layout learnable without changing inference. We propose a multimodal encoder that augments local patch representations with a global layout embedding, trained via textual descriptions encoding document layout information. Across four ViDoRe-v2 datasets, our model improves over the strongest architecturally comparable ColPali/ColQwen baseline by +2.4 nDCG@5 and +2.3 MAP@5, with statistically significant per-dataset gains over ColQwen.

</details>


### [37] [NICE FACT: Diagnosing and Calibrating VLMs in Quantitative Reasoning for Kinematic Physics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08452)
*Jian Lan,Zhicheng Liu,Xinpeng Wang,Yuhao Zhou,Haokun Chen,Jiancheng Lv,Barbara Plank,Thomas Seidl*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 这篇论文提出了NICE和FACT双重诊断范式，用于评估视觉语言模型在物理世界感知和物理定律运用方面的能力，特别是针对运动学物理的定量推理。研究发现当前先进模型在视觉保真度、物理定律理解和时间基础方面存在严重缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型在空间智能任务（如物理推理）中表现不佳，缺乏科学的分析来揭示模型是真正推理还是猜测。社区迫切需要理解VLMs如何感知物理世界、运用物理定律，并评估模型置信度的可靠性。

Method: 提出了NICE和FACT双重诊断范式：FACT诊断视觉保真度、物理定律理解和时间基础；NICE研究新颖的邻域感知校准方法和评估指标，用于评估和校准置信度的可靠性。在6个最新的先进VLM上进行了评估。

Result: 研究发现模型无法识别视觉前提条件或运用必要的物理定律来得出答案。在运动学物理的定量推理任务中，模型表现存在系统性缺陷。

Conclusion: 这项工作建立了一个标准化的诊断范式，为指导开发可信、物理基础的视觉语言模型提供了重要参考。强调了当前模型在物理世界理解和推理方面的局限性，为未来研究方向提供了指导。

Abstract: The ability to derive precise spatial and physical insights is a cornerstone of vision-language models (VLMs), yet their poor performances in related spatial intelligence tasks such as physical reasoning remain a fundamental barrier. The community critically lacks a scientific analysis revealing whether VLMs faithfully reach answers or plausibly make guesses. This work aims to provide a fundamental understanding of how VLMs perceive the physical world, and utilize physical laws, while assessing the reliability of model confidence. We propose NICE and FACT, a dual-diagnostic paradigm that explicitly decomposes quantitative reasoning for kinematic physics: FACT diagnoses visual fidelity, physical law comprehension, and temporal grounding. NICE studies our novel neighborhood-informed calibration method and novel metrics to evaluate and calibrate confidence reliability. Evaluated across 6 latest state-of-the-art VLMs, we uncover that models fail to identify visual preconditions or utilize necessary physical laws to reach answers. This work highlights and establishes a standardized diagnostic paradigm to guide the development of faithful, physically-grounded VLMs.

</details>


### [38] [CapCLIP: A Vision-Language Representation Alignment Approach for Wireless Capsule Endoscopy Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08493)
*Haroon Wahab,Irfan Mehmood,Hassan Ugail*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CapCLIP：一个针对无线胶囊内窥镜的领域特定视觉-语言表示学习框架，通过对齐胶囊内窥镜图像与临床文本描述来学习既语义丰富又具有迁移性的嵌入，在零样本条件下显著优于现有基础模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无线胶囊内窥镜产生的图像量大且图像条件多变，现有基于学习的方法多为纯视觉模型，局限于狭窄的病理集，在不同数据集和中心间的迁移能力有限。

Method: 提出CapCLIP框架，将胶囊内窥镜图像与基于标准化术语和病理感知字幕模板的临床文本描述对齐，学习语义丰富且可迁移的嵌入表示。在严格零样本条件下与相关开源视觉和视觉-语言基础模型进行比较评估。

Result: CapCLIP在三个下游任务（K最近邻分类、CLIP式图像-文本分类、文本-图像检索）中一致优于基线模型，特别是在零样本图像-文本分类和跨模态检索任务中，在分布外数据集上表现出显著优势。

Conclusion: 语言引导的表示学习可以改善无线胶囊内窥镜分析中的泛化能力和语义可解释性，CapCLIP是为胶囊内窥镜定制的专用基础模型的探索方向，支持语言锚定的WCE分析方法。

Abstract: Wireless capsule endoscopy (WCE) enables non-invasive visual assessment of the small bowel, but its clinical utility is constrained by the large volume of frames generated per examination and the difficulty of recognising subtle abnormalities under highly variable imaging conditions. Existing learning-based approaches for WCE are predominantly vision-only, often confined to narrow pathology sets, and show limited transfer across datasets and centres. To address these limitations, this study introduces CapCLIP, a domain-specific vision-language representation learning framework for WCE. CapCLIP aligns capsule endoscopy frames with clinically grounded textual descriptions derived from standardised nomenclature and pathology-aware caption templates, thereby learning embeddings that are both semantically informed and transferable. The proposed framework is evaluated against relevant open-source vision and vision-language foundation models under strict zero-shot conditions using unseen WCE datasets. Evaluation covers three downstream tasks: K-nearest neighbour classification, CLIP-style image-text classification, and text-to-image retrieval. Across these settings, CapCLIP consistently outperforms the compared baselines, with particularly strong gains in zero-shot image-text classification and cross-modal retrieval on out-of-distribution datasets. The results indicate that language-guided representation learning can improve both generalisation and semantic interpretability in WCE analysis. These findings position CapCLIP as a step toward foundation models tailored to capsule endoscopy and support the use of language-grounded WCE analysis.

</details>


### [39] [Geometric Flood Depth Estimation: Fusing Transformer-Based Segmentation with Digital Elevation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08521)
*Nhut Le,Ehsan Karimi,Maryam Rahnemoonfar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于单目航空影像的洪水深度估计方法，通过结合2D语义分割和数字高程模型来计算水位高程和洪水深度，无需流体动力学模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灾后态势感知需要了解洪水范围和体积，但传统的2D语义分割缺乏垂直维度信息，无法评估通航性和结构风险。现有的流体动力学模拟存在延迟问题。

Method: 采用Mask2Former（基于Transformer的先进分割模型）生成精确的2D洪水掩码，将其与数字高程模型融合，识别水陆边界，计算全局水位高程（Z_water），并基于局部静水平衡原理计算每个像素的洪水深度。

Result: 使用FloodNet和CRASAR-U-DROIDS数据集评估该方法，证明高性能分割可以从2D影像中提取3D体积数据，无需流体动力学模拟的延迟。

Conclusion: 该方法成功地将几何水位高程方法应用于单目航空影像的洪水深度估计，为灾后态势感知提供了从2D分割到3D体积数据提取的有效解决方案。

Abstract: Post-disaster situational awareness relies heavily on understanding both the extent and the volume of floodwaters. While 2D semantic segmentation provides accurate flood masking, it lacks the vertical dimension required to assess navigability and structural risk. This paper presents a geometric "Water Surface Elevation" approach for estimating flood depth from monocular aerial imagery. Our pipeline utilizes Mask2Former, a state-of-the-art transformer-based segmentation model, to generate precise 2D flood masks. These masks are fused with Digital Elevation Models (DEMs) to identify the water-land boundary, calculate a global water surface elevation ($Z_{water}$), and compute per-pixel depth based on the principle of local hydrostatic equilibrium. We evaluate this workflow using the FloodNet and CRASAR-U-DROIDS datasets, demonstrating how high-performance segmentation can be leveraged to extract 3D volumetric data from 2D imagery without the latency of hydrodynamic simulations.

</details>


### [40] [ZAYA1-VL-8B Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08560)
*Hassan Shapourian,Kasra Hejazi,Olabode M. Sule,Beren Millidge*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ZAYA1-VL-8B 是一个紧凑的混合专家视觉语言模型，基于自研语言模型 ZAYA1-8B 构建，在9.2B总参数中只有1.4B活跃参数，性能可媲美主流基础模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发一个紧凑但高性能的视觉语言模型，在保持模型规模较小的同时，实现与大型基础模型竞争的性能。

Method: 采用混合专家架构，包含两项关键创新：1) 在LLM中集成视觉特定LoRA适配器，在不增加专家数量的情况下增强模态特定能力；2) 在LLM中对图像标记应用双向注意力机制以增强视觉理解。详细介绍了包括数据组成、序列打包和注意力掩码方案在内的完整训练流程。

Result: ZAYA1-VL-8B 在多种图像理解、推理和计数基准测试中，性能与领先的基础模型如Molmo2-4B和InternVL3.5-4B相当，并超过了Qwen2.5-VL-3B、PLM-3B和MolmoE-1B等模型。模型总参数9.2B，活跃参数1.4B（包括视觉编码器）。

Conclusion: 成功开发了一个参数高效的紧凑视觉语言模型，通过创新的架构设计实现了与更大模型竞争的性能，并在Hugging Face上公开提供。

Abstract: We present ZAYA1-VL-8B, a compact mixture-of-experts vision-language model built upon our in-house language model, ZAYA1-8B. Despite its compact size, ZAYA1-VL achieves performance competitive with leading base models such as Molmo2-4B and InternVL3.5-4B, while surpassing models including Qwen2.5-VL-3B, PLM-3B, and MolmoE-1B across a range of image understanding, reasoning, and counting benchmarks. The architecture incorporates two key innovations: (1) vision-specific LoRA adapters integrated into the LLM to increase modality-specific capacity without increasing the number of experts, and (2) bidirectional attention over image tokens within the LLM to enhance visual understanding. We detail the full training pipeline including data composition at each stage, sequence packing, and the attention masking scheme. The model comprises 9.2B total parameters, with 1.4B active parameters including the vision encoder, and is publicly available at https://huggingface.co/Zyphra/ZAYA1-VL.

</details>


### [41] [MicroDiffuse3D: A Foundation Model for 3D Microscopy Imaging Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08566)
*Yongkang Li,Brian Wong,King Wai Chiu,Hanwen Xu,Tangqi Fang,Erin Dunnington,Dan Fu,Sheng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MicroDiffuse3D是一个预训练的基础模型，用于三维显微图像恢复，能够从低分辨率测量中重建高质量的体积结构，显著提高化学成像的吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 化学成像虽然能提供直接的生化信息，但其广泛应用受到三维成像数据采集速度慢的限制，尤其是在需要高吞吐量的应用中，如术中诊断和药物反应分析。

Method: 提出了MicroDiffuse3D预训练基础模型，通过处理退化的低分辨率测量数据来恢复高质量的三维体积结构，实现了显著提高的成像吞吐量。

Result: 在三个具有挑战性的恢复设置中评估：1）16倍体积稀疏下的三维超分辨率；2）分辨率和噪声联合退化；3）低信噪比下的三维去噪。在稀疏三维超分辨率设置中，实现了分割质量提高10.58%，线轮廓一致性提高15.59%，并在所有设置中都优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: 预训练的三维恢复模型为克服体积化学成像中的吞吐量和信噪比限制提供了广泛适用的策略，使得在高分辨率下实现以前难以达到的规模和速度进行分析成为可能。

Abstract: Chemical imaging enables label-free visualization of cells, tissues and living systems while providing direct biochemical information that is difficult to obtain with conventional fluorescence microscopy. Despite its promise in applications ranging from intraoperative diagnosis to drug-response analysis, its broader use remains limited by slow data acquisition, particularly for three-dimensional imaging. Here we present MicroDiffuse3D, a pretrained foundation model for 3D microscopy image restoration that recovers high-quality volumetric structure from degraded low-resolution measurements acquired at substantially higher throughput. We evaluated MicroDiffuse3D across three challenging restoration settings, including 3D super-resolution under 16-fold volumetric sparsity, joint degradation in resolution and noise, and 3D denoising in the low signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) regime, where the model delivered clear gains over strong baselines. Under the sparse 3D super-resolution setting, MicroDiffuse3D produced clearer continuity across depth with fewer artifacts and improved segmentation quality by 10.58% and line-profile concordance by 15.59%. Together, our results establish pretrained 3D restoration as a broadly applicable strategy for overcoming the throughput and SNR limitations in volumetric chemical imaging, enabling high-resolution analysis at scales and speeds that were previously difficult to achieve.

</details>


### [42] [ACWM-Phys: Investigating Generalized Physical Interaction in Action-Conditioned Video World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08567)
*Haotian Xue,Yipu Chen,Liqian Ma,Zelin Zhao,Lama Moukheiber,Yuchen Zhu,Yongxin Che*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ACWM-Phys是一个用于评估动作条件世界模型在多样化物理动力学中预测能力的新基准，包含刚性体动力学、运动学、可变形物体交互和粒子动力学，通过系统性实验揭示了模型泛化能力与任务复杂性之间的关键关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动作条件世界模型（ACWMs）的基准主要集中在自我中心导航或狭窄的任务特定机器人数据集上，缺乏对广义世界理解所需的丰富物理交互的覆盖。这限制了评估模型在多样化物理动力学中的预测和泛化能力。

Method: 研究者构建了ACWM-Phys基准：1）在可控仿真环境中设计精心规划的动作空间；2）包含训练和评估数据，涵盖刚性体动力学、运动学、可变形物体交互和粒子动力学；3）设计分布内和分布外评估协议，控制交互模式或场景配置的变化；4）通过ACWM-DiT模型进行系统性实验和消融研究。

Result: 系统性实验表明：1）分布外泛化不仅取决于物理机制，还与有效任务复杂性相关；2）模型在视觉简单、低维交互且具有清晰几何结构的情况下泛化良好；3）在可变形接触、高维控制和复杂铰接运动上性能下降更大；4）模型仍依赖视觉外观模式而非完全学习底层物理；5）交叉注意力改善高维动作条件；6）因果VAE优于逐帧编码器；7）更大的动作空间更难建模但通过提供更丰富的控制信号可改善泛化。

Conclusion: ACWM-Phys基准通过可控仿真环境实现了精确数据收集、可重复评估和系统性分析，为物理基础世界模型的设计提供了指导。研究发现模型泛化能力与任务复杂性密切相关，这为开发更鲁棒的动作条件世界模型提供了重要见解。

Abstract: Action-conditioned world models (ACWMs) have shown strong promise for video prediction and decision-making. However, existing benchmarks are largely restricted to egocentric navigation or narrow, task-specific robotics datasets, offering only limited coverage of the rich physical interactions required for generalized world understanding. We introduce ACWM-Phys, a new benchmark for evaluating action-conditioned prediction under diverse physical dynamics in a clean, controllable simulation environment with a carefully designed action space. ACWM-Phys contains training and evaluation data spanning rigid-body dynamics, kinematics, deformable-object interactions, and particle dynamics. To evaluate both interpolation and generalization, we design in-distribution and out-of-distribution protocols with controlled shifts in interaction patterns or scene configurations. By building the benchmark in a fully controllable simulator, ACWM-Phys enables precise data collection, reproducible evaluation, and systematic analysis of model capabilities for physically grounded world modeling. Through systematic experiments on ACWM-DiT, we find that OoD generalization depends not only on the physical regime but also on effective task complexity: models generalize well on visually simple, low-dimensional interactions with clear geometric structure, but suffer larger drops on deformable contacts, high-dimensional control, and complex articulated motion. This suggests that the model still relies heavily on visual appearance patterns instead of fully learning the underlying physics. Ablations show that cross-attention improves high-dimensional action conditioning, causal VAEs outperform frame-wise encoders, and larger action spaces are harder to model but can improve generalization by providing richer control signals. These findings guide the design of physically grounded world models.

</details>


### [43] [Enhancing Consistency Models for Multi-Agent Trajectory Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08572)
*Alen Mrdovic,Qingze,Liu,Danrui Li,Mathew Schwartz,Kaidong Hu,Sejong Yoon,Mubbasir Kapadia,Vladimir Pavlovic*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ECTraj是一个增强的一致性模型框架，用于多智能体轨迹预测，通过改进的训练机制和条件生成实现高质量的单步推理，在Argoverse 2数据集上建立了新的性能基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于扩散模型的多智能体轨迹预测方法受到迭代去噪过程的限制，导致推理延迟过高，无法满足自动驾驶等时间关键应用的需求。虽然DDIM和优化的初始噪声分布可以部分缓解这一问题，但仍无法实现真正的单步生成，或者受到所选噪声分布的限制。

Method: 提出ECTraj增强一致性模型流水线，扩展了师生一致性训练方案：学生模型产生标准输出，教师模型显式融合其预测与部分真实轨迹数据以提供更强的监督信号。同时利用一致性模型的直接去噪特性，在训练期间实现top-K多模态生成。

Result: 在Argoverse 2大规模数据集上，ECTraj结合条件生成与增强的一致性目标，实现了更快的推理速度和改进的预测精度，建立了具有竞争力的新性能基准。

Conclusion: ECTraj框架成功解决了多智能体轨迹预测中扩散模型的推理延迟问题，通过一致性模型的直接映射特性和改进的训练机制，实现了高质量的单步生成，为时间关键应用提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Diffusion models for multi-agent trajectory prediction are limited by iterative denoising, which causes inference latency that hinders their use in time-critical settings like autonomous driving. Fast-sampling variants using DDIM and informed initial noise distributions partially alleviate this issue, but they either fail to achieve true single-step generation or are constrained by the chosen noise distribution. Consistency Models (CMs) offer high-quality one-step generation by mapping noise directly to data, but are difficult to train from scratch . We propose ECTraj, an enhanced CM pipeline with improved training and conditional generation for trajectory prediction. Our framework extends the student-teacher consistency training scheme: the student produces standard outputs, while the teacher explicitly fuses its predictions with parts of the ground truth to give stronger supervision. We also exploit CMs' direct denoising for top-K multi-shot generation during training. Combining conditional generation with this enhanced consistency objective yields faster inference and improved prediction accuracy, establishing competitive new benchmarks on the large-scale Argoverse 2 dataset.

</details>


### [44] [Improving Generative Adversarial Networks with Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08577)
*Antoni Nowinowski,Krzysztof Krawiec*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SD-GAN通过将EMA生成器作为教师模型来指导活跃生成器（学生），使用感知损失，从而改进GAN训练稳定性和图像质量


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在现代GAN中，虽然EMA权重平均模型比活跃训练的生成器表现更好，但它仅在最终部署时使用，不参与训练过程，这是一个被错失的机会。作者希望利用EMA模型的优势来改进训练过程

Method: 提出自蒸馏GAN（SD-GAN），将EMA生成器作为教师模型，活跃生成器作为学生模型，通过感知损失让教师指导学生。在Dirac-GAN设置中证明了局部渐近稳定性，并展示了它能抑制传统GAN中的寄生循环行为

Result: 在多种架构和数据集上的实验表明，SD-GAN在FID和random-FID等指标上提高了最终图像质量，稳定了优化轨迹，提供了与传统对抗损失不相关的额外学习指导，并证明对预训练GAN模型的微调也有效

Conclusion: SD-GAN通过将EMA生成器作为教师模型来指导活跃生成器，有效利用了EMA的优势，改进了GAN训练的稳定性和图像生成质量，是一种有前景的GAN训练方法

Abstract: In modern GANs, maintaining an Exponential Moving Average (EMA) of the generator's weights is a standard practice, as such an averaged model consistently outperforms the actively trained generator. However, the EMA generator is used for final deployment only and does not influence the training process. To address this missed opportunity, we introduce Self-Distilled GAN (SD-GAN) that employs the EMA generator as a teacher to guide the active generator (student) via perceptual loss. We prove the local asymptotic stability of SD-GAN in the Dirac-GAN setting and show that it dampens the parasitic cycling behavior that plagues the conventional GANs. Empirical evaluations across established architectures and datasets demonstrate that SD-GAN improves the final image quality on several metrics (FID and random-FID in particular), stabilizes the optimization trajectory and provides additional learning guidance that is not trivially correlated with the conventional adversarial loss. It also proves effective for fine-tuning pretrained GAN models.

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

### [45] [Sanity Checks for Long-Form Hallucination Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08346)
*Geigh Zollicoffer,Minh Vu,Hongli Zhan,Raymond Li,Manish Bhattarai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个控制不变性方法来检测大型语言模型的幻觉，区分推理过程评估与表面答案相关性的利用，并展示了TRACTS轻量级评分器在不依赖复杂学习表示的情况下实现有效检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前基于思维链推理轨迹的大语言模型幻觉检测方法存在一个根本问题：不清楚这些方法是真正评估推理过程本身，还是仅仅利用最终答案的表面相关性。需要区分检测方法的预测能力是来自答案级别的伪迹，还是来自中间推理的结构或有效性。

Method: 提出了控制不变性方法，通过两种预言测试来揭示这种区别：1) Force测试：将每个响应的最终答案替换为真实答案，同时保留推理轨迹；2) Remove测试：剥离答案宣布步骤，同时保持轨迹完整。此外，提出了TRACT评分器，这是一个基于词汇轨迹特征的轻量级评分器，包括：对冲趋势、步骤长度动态和跨响应词汇收敛。

Result: 研究表明，一旦控制答案伪迹，有效的检测并不一定需要复杂的学习表示：TRACT评分器仅使用词汇轨迹特征就能实现强大的鲁棒性，同时在未扰动轨迹上竞争性或优于现有基线方法。控制不变性方法能够有效区分检测方法是否真正利用推理信号。

Conclusion: 当前推理感知幻觉检测的核心挑战不是轨迹中缺乏信号，而是未能将其从终点线索中分离出来。通过控制不变性方法可以暴露这一区别，而轻量级基于特征的评分器如TRACT能够有效地隔离推理信号，为更鲁棒的幻觉检测提供了新方向。

Abstract: Hallucination detection methods for large language models increasingly operate on chain-of-thought reasoning traces, yet it remains unclear whether they evaluate the reasoning itself or merely exploit surface correlates of the final answer. We introduce a controlled-invariance methodology that exposes this distinction through two oracle tests: \textsc{Force}, which replaces each response's final answer with the ground truth while preserving the reasoning trace, and \textsc{Remove}, which strips answer-announcement steps while leaving the trajectory intact. This reveals if their predictive power derives from answer-level artifacts rather than from the structure or validity of intermediate reasoning. We further show that once these artifacts are controlled for, effective detection does not necessarily require complex learned representations: TRACT, a lightweight scorer built on lexical trajectory features (hedging trends, step-length dynamics, and cross-response vocabulary convergence), achieves strong robustness while remaining competitive with or outperforming existing baselines on unperturbed traces. These findings suggest that the current central challenge in reasoning-aware hallucination detection is not the absence of signal in the trace, but the failure to isolate it from endpoint cues.

</details>


### [46] [How Much Do Circuits Tell Us? Measuring the Consistency and Specificity of Language Model Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08348)
*Michael Li,Nishant Subramani*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 通过边属性修补分析跨任务和模型的电路重用，发现电路内共享组件对任务性能至关重要，但电路缺乏任务特异性，跨任务电路重叠显著，这挑战了通过电路实现针对性理解和干预的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究机械可解释性中电路框架的两个较少被研究的特性：一致性（组件在任务内的重复出现）和特异性（组件对特定任务的独特性）。目标是了解电路在多大程度上能支持对模型行为的针对性理解和干预。

Method: 使用边属性修补方法，在六个任务和七个模型上分析电路重用。测量电路重用率（任务内示例电路共享组件的比例），并通过消融实验评估组件的必要性和任务特异性。

Result: 1) 任务内电路重用率很高，共享组件对任务性能至关重要（消融导致相对准确率下降高达~100%）。2) 电路缺乏任务特异性：消融一个任务的电路对其他任务性能的损害与该任务自身电路的消融相似。3) 跨任务电路存在显著重叠，这些重叠组件对性能具有因果重要性。4) 少数电路包含较小的任务特定组件集，但这些仅占电路性能的较小部分。

Conclusion: 虽然在注意力头和MLP层级别的电路发现能识别重要组件，但其缺乏任务特异性引发了对电路在多大程度上能支持针对性理解和干预模型行为的质疑。电路可能更多地反映通用的计算模式而非特定任务的专门机制。

Abstract: The circuits framework in mechanistic interpretability aims to identify causally important sparse subgraphs of model components, typically evaluated by measuring necessity and sufficiency. We measure circuit reuse, the proportion of components shared across per-example circuits within a task, and investigate two less-studied properties of this: consistency, the recurrence of components within a task, and specificity, their uniqueness to a task. Using edge attribution patching across six tasks and seven models, we find that within-task reuse is high and that shared components are necessary for task performance, with ablations causing up to $\sim$100% relative accuracy drops. However, circuits turn out not to be task-specific: ablating one task's circuit damages another task's performance about as much as that task's own circuit does. We discover that this is due to substantial overlap between circuits across tasks, which are causally important for performance. Some circuits do contain a smaller set of task-specific components, but these account for only a modest portion of circuit performance. Overall, our findings suggest that while circuit discovery at the level of attention heads and MLP layers identifies important components, their lack of task-specificity raises questions about the degree to which circuits can support targeted understanding and intervention on model behavior.

</details>


### [47] [Change My View? The Dynamics of Persuasion and Polarization in Online Discourse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08383)
*David Freeborn,Malihe Alikani,Anthony Sicilia*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 使用大型语言模型分析Reddit r/ChangeMyView辩论语料库，发现让步、共情等修辞策略能显著提高观点改变的可能性，而正面反驳、可信度攻击和话题转移则会降低这种可能性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 哲学观点认为共享证据和理性论证应该导致观点趋同，但日常讨论往往并非如此。研究者希望探究在公共辩论中哪些修辞策略实际有效促进观点改变，以理解理性对话的实际运作机制。

Method: 使用大型语言模型分析Reddit r/ChangeMyView平台的辩论语料库，让LLM在半程时预测观点改变的可能性作为基线。通过混合机器辅助程序对每个回复编码十种常见修辞策略（让步、共情、逻辑挑战、可信度诉求等），并将这些策略特征添加到预测模型中。

Result: 添加修辞策略特征显著提高了预测能力。研究发现：表达让步或共情对齐的行动大幅增加观点改变的可能性，而正面反驳、可信度攻击和话题转移则减少观点改变的可能性。有效公共推理不仅依赖证据内容，也依赖于关系框架。

Conclusion: 有效公共推理同等依赖于关系框架和证据内容。研究发现挑战了仅关注证据共享的规范理性对话理论，邀请对理性对话的规范理论进行细化，强调修辞策略在促进观点改变中的重要作用。

Abstract: Philosophical accounts of persuasion often assume that shared evidence and rational argumentation should lead to a convergence of views between peers, yet everyday discourse often suggests otherwise. In this study, we use large language models to analyze a corpus of debates on Reddit's r/ChangeMyView, where belief revision is publicly signaled. Large language models were asked, halfway through each discussion, to forecast whether such an acknowledgement would arise; their probabilistic estimates serve as a conversational baseline. Each reply was then coded, through a hybrid machine-assisted procedure, for ten familiar rhetorical strategies -- concession, empathy, logical challenge, credibility appeals, and so forth. Adding these strategic features markedly improves predictive power and yields a consistent pattern: moves that express concession or empathetic alignment substantially increase the prospect of belief change, whereas frontal refutation, credibility attacks, and topic deflection diminish it. The findings indicate that effective public reasoning depends as much on relational framing as on evidential content, and they invite a refinement of normative accounts of rational dialogue.

</details>


### [48] [jina-embeddings-v5-omni: Text-Geometry-Preserving Multimodal Embeddings via Frozen-Tower Composition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08384)
*Florian Hönicke,Michael Günther,Andreas Koukounas,Kalim Akram,Scott Martens,Saba Sturua,Han Xiao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出冻结编码器模型组合方法，通过扩展现有文本嵌入模型来支持多模态输入，仅训练0.35%的参数即可创建统一的文本、图像、音频和视频嵌入空间


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型需要大量参数训练来支持多模态嵌入，资源消耗大且难以保持文本嵌入的原始性能。需要一种高效的方法将文本嵌入模型扩展到多模态领域，同时保持文本输入的嵌入质量不变。

Method: 采用VLM风格架构，基于已有的Jina Embeddings v5文本模型，添加冻结的图像和音频编码器，仅训练连接组件（占联合模型总权重的0.35%）。核心文本嵌入模型和非文本媒体编码器保持冻结状态，语言模型基本不变。

Result: 开发了jina-embeddings-v5-omni套件，能够在单一语义嵌入空间中编码文本、图像、音频和视频输入。评估显示该方法与最先进的多模态嵌入模型性能相当，文本输入的嵌入与原始Jina Embeddings v5模型完全相同。

Conclusion: 冻结编码器模型组合是一种高效的多模态嵌入方法，通过极少的参数训练即可获得竞争性的性能，同时保持文本嵌入的向后兼容性，为多模态应用提供了一种资源友好的解决方案。

Abstract: In this work, we introduce frozen-encoder model composition, a novel approach to multimodal embedding models. We build on the VLM-style architecture, in which non-text encoders are adapted to produce input for a language model, which in turn generates embeddings for all varieties of input. We present the result: the jina-embeddings-v5-omni suite, a pair of models that encode text, image, audio, and video input into a single semantic embedding space. Our method is to extend the two Jina Embeddings v5 Text models to support additional media by adding encoders for images and audio. The backbone text embedding models and the added non-text media encoders remain frozen. We only trained the connecting components, representing 0.35% of the total weights of the joint model. Training is therefore much more efficient than full-parameter retraining. Additionally, the language model remains effectively unaltered, producing exactly the same embeddings for text inputs as the Jina Embeddings v5 Text models. Our evaluations show that this approach produces results that are competitive with the state-of-the-art, yielding nearly equal performance to larger multimodal embedding models.

</details>


### [49] [AIPO: : Learning to Reason from Active Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08401)
*Junnan Liu,Linhao Luo,Thuy-Trang Vu,Gholamreza Haffari*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: AIPO是一个增强型强化学习框架，通过主动多智能体交互来提升大语言模型的推理能力。该框架在训练期间允许策略模型在遇到推理瓶颈时主动咨询三个协作智能体，从而精细扩展其能力边界，最终让策略模型能够独立进行推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的强化学习算法面临一个基本限制：其探索能力受限于策略模型的固有能力边界。虽然近期方法引入了外部专家演示来扩展这一边界，但它们通常依赖完整的轨迹级指导，这存在样本效率低、信息稀疏且可能将探索限制在静态指导空间的问题。

Method: 提出了AIPO框架，该框架通过主动多智能体交互扩展策略模型的能力边界。具体来说，当策略模型遇到推理瓶颈时，可以主动咨询三个功能协作智能体：验证智能体、知识智能体和推理智能体。同时引入了定制的重要性采样系数和裁剪策略，以减轻从智能体反馈学习时出现的离策略偏差和梯度消失问题。

Result: 在AIME、MATH500、GPQA-Diamond和LiveCodeBench等多样化推理基准测试中，AIPO一致地提高了推理性能，在不同的策略模型和RLVR算法上表现出强大的泛化能力，并有效扩展了策略模型的推理能力边界。

Conclusion: AIPO框架通过主动多智能体交互成功克服了现有强化学习方法的限制，提供了一种更高效、精细的能力边界扩展方法，最终使策略模型能够独立进行高质量推理，为提升LLM推理能力提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable reasoning capabilities, largely stimulated by Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR). However, existing RL algorithms face a fundamental limitation: their exploration remains largely constrained by the inherent capability boundary of the policy model. Although recent methods introduce external expert demonstrations to extend this boundary, they typically rely on complete trajectory-level guidance, which is sample-inefficient, information-sparse, and may confine exploration to a static guidance space. Inspired by the potential of multi-agent systems, we propose $\textbf{AIPO}$, an enhanced reinforcement learning framework that improves LLM reasoning through active multi-agent interaction during exploration. Specifically, AIPO enables the policy model to proactively consult three functional collaborative agents, $\textit{Verify Agent}$, $\textit{Knowledge Agent}$, and $\textit{Reasoning Agent}$, when encountering reasoning bottlenecks, thereby receiving fine-grained and targeted guidance to actively expand its capability boundary during training. We further introduce a tailored importance sampling coefficient together with a clipping strategy to mitigate the off-policy bias and gradient vanishing issues that arise when learning from agent-provided feedback. After training, the policy model performs reasoning independently without relying on collaborative agents. Extensive experiments on diverse reasoning benchmarks, including AIME, MATH500, GPQA-Diamond, and LiveCodeBench, show that AIPO consistently improves reasoning performance, generalizes robustly across different policy models and RLVR algorithms, and effectively expands the reasoning capability boundary of the policy model.

</details>


### [50] [Built Environment Reasoning from Remote Sensing Imagery Using Large Vision--Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08404)
*Dongdong Wang,Deepak Balakrishnan,Ravi Srinivasan,Shenhao Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 利用大型语言模型处理遥感影像，为智慧城市提供建成环境特征分析、设计建议、可建造性评估等任务的多模态解决方案


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索如何整合大型语言模型与遥感影像来提升智慧城市决策支持能力，特别是在建成环境特征描述、设计建议生成、土地利用模式分析和风险识别等方面的应用潜力

Method: 采用多空间尺度的遥感影像作为多模态语言模型的输入，研究不同尺度影像对建成环境相关推理的影响；比较InternVL和Qwen等先进LLM在准确性和可靠性方面的表现

Result: 结果显示整合遥感影像与大型语言模型具有显著潜力，能够有效辅助智慧城市决策制定和建成环境相关任务

Conclusion: 多模态语言模型与遥感影像的结合为智慧城市应用提供了新的技术路径，在建成环境分析、设计建议生成等方面展现出实用价值

Abstract: This work investigates the use of large language models (LLMs) for tasks in smart cities. The core idea is to leverage remote sensing imagery to characterize the built environment, including design suggestions, constructability assessment, landuse patterns, and risk identification. We examine remote sensing imagery at multiple spatial scales as inputs for multimodal language modeling and evaluate their effects on built-environment-related reasoning. In addition, we compare state-of-the-art LLMs, including InternVL and Qwen, in terms of accuracy and reliability when generating built environment recommendations. The results demonstrate the potential of integrating remote sensing imagery with large language models to assist smart cities and decision-making.

</details>


### [51] [Effective Explanations Support Planning Under Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08406)
*Hanqi Zhou,Britt Besch,Charley M. Wu,Tobias Gerstenberg*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一个计算模型来评估程序性解释的质量：用大语言模型将解释转化为行动规划，通过规划智能体在部分可观测环境下执行，根据路径效率和可靠性对解释进行评分


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释如何从A点到B点具有挑战性，需要基于听众听到的内容来模拟他们的行为。目前缺乏系统评估解释质量的计算框架，特别是在不确定性和部分可观测环境下的行动指导

Method: 提出一个计算模型：1）用大语言模型将自然语言解释转化为程序化指导（策略先验和价值图）；2）规划智能体在部分可观测环境下执行该指导；3）基于路径效率和可靠性对解释进行评分，惩罚重新规划；4）通过四个预注册实验，收集1200条解释，获取有用性判断和导航行为数据

Result: 高分解释被判断为更有用，并能改善导航：获得解释的参与者表现优于没有解释的参与者，高评分解释比低评分解释帮助更大。实验结果支持模型的有效性

Conclusion: 程序性解释是效用导向的沟通形式，其质量取决于语言如何在不确定性下被转化为实际行动。提出的计算框架为评估和优化解释质量提供了系统性方法

Abstract: Explaining how to get from A to B can be challenging. It requires mentally simulating what the listener will do based on what they are told. To capture this process, we propose a computational model that converts utterances into action plans: a large language model translates an explanation into program-like guidance (a policy prior and value map), and a planning agent executes it under partial observability. We score explanations by the efficiency and reliability of the resulting paths, penalizing replanning. Across four preregistered experiments, we collect a corpus of 1,200 explanations over 24 maps, elicit helpfulness judgments, measure baseline navigation, and test behavior with explanations of differing quality. Higher-scored explanations are judged more helpful and improve navigation: participants with explanations outperform those without, and high-scoring explanations help more than low-scoring ones. Together, these results show procedural explanation as utility-guided communication shaped by how language can be grounded into action under uncertainty.

</details>


### [52] [A Semantic-Sampling Framework for Evaluating Calibration in Open-Ended Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08432)
*Zhanliang Wang,Jiancong Xiao,Ruochen Jin,Shu Yang,Bojian Hou,Li Shen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Sem-ECE：面向开放问答的语义采样期望校准误差评估框架，通过采样答案、语义分组和频率置信度来解决现有校准评估方法的不足


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在高风险领域的可靠部署需要校准评估，但现有方法在开放问答场景下存在局限：基于logit的方法需要受限输出格式和内部概率；口头置信度易过度自信；基于采样的方法依赖任务特定的提取规则且缺乏清晰的有限样本目标

Method: 提出Sem-ECE框架，采样模型答案、将答案分组到语义类别、使用频率作为置信度。包含两个估计器：Sem1-ECE（同样本自一致性评分）和Sem2-ECE（留出变体），后者将答案选择与置信度评估分离

Result: 理论证明两个估计器均渐近无偏，在简单问题上结果一致，在困难问题上Sem2-ECE具有更小的校准误差。三个开放问答基准和五个商业LLM的实验验证了理论预测，Sem-ECE优于口头置信度和现有采样方法，当内部概率不可用时补充基于logit的评估

Conclusion: Sem-ECE为开放问答提供了有效的校准评估框架，克服了现有方法的局限性，通过语义采样方法能够更准确地评估LLM的校准性能，且两个估计器之间的差异可作为问题难度的诊断指标

Abstract: Calibration measures whether a model's predicted confidence aligns with its empirical accuracy, and is central to the reliable deployment of large language models (LLMs) in high-stakes domains such as medicine and law. While much recent work focuses on improving LLM calibration, the equally important question of how to evaluate it in realistic settings remains underdeveloped. Open-ended question answering (QA), the most common deployment setting for modern LLMs, is where existing evaluation methods fall short: logit-based metrics need restricted output formats and internal probabilities; verbalized confidence is self-reported and often overconfident; and sampling-based methods rely on task-specific extraction rules without a clear finite-sample target. We introduce Sem-ECE (Semantic-Sampling Expected Calibration Error), a calibration evaluation framework for open-ended QA that samples answers from the model, groups them into semantic classes, and uses the resulting frequencies as confidence. We study two estimators within this framework: Sem$_1$-ECE, the same-sample self-consistency score, and Sem$_2$-ECE, a held-out variant that separates answer selection from confidence evaluation. We prove both are asymptotically unbiased, and further show that they agree on easy questions but diverge on hard ones with Sem$_2$ achieving strictly smaller calibration error, so their gap also serves as a diagnostic for question difficulty. Experiments on three open-ended QA benchmarks across five leading commercial LLMs match our theoretical predictions and show that Sem-ECE outperforms verbalized confidence and existing sampling-based methods, while complementing logit-based evaluation when internal probabilities are unavailable.

</details>


### [53] [Magis-Bench: Evaluating LLMs on Magistrate-Level Legal Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08437)
*Ramon Pires,Thales Sales Almeida,Celio Larcher Junior,Giovana Bonás,Hugo Abonizio,Marcos Piau,Roseval Malaquias Junior,Thiago Laitz,Rodrigo Nogueira*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Magis-Bench：一个基于巴西司法考试题目的法律AI基准测试，评估LLM在裁判文书写作和司法推理方面的能力，结果显示即使最佳模型也未能达到70%的最高分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的法律AI基准主要关注法律论证或文件生成任务，但评估法律论证（权衡竞争主张、将法律原则应用于事实、做出合理裁决）的能力与辩护能力同样重要，这是法律系统有效运行的基础。

Method: 引入Magis-Bench基准，包含74个来自2023-2025年巴西司法职位竞争性考试的题目，包括多轮结构化法律分析问题和需要撰写完整民事/刑事判决书的实践练习；使用LLM-as-a-judge方法，以四个独立的前沿模型作为评估者，对23个最先进的LLM进行评估。

Result: 评估显示强烈的评委间一致性（Kendall's W = 0.984；成对Kendall's τ ≥ 0.897）；Google的Gemini-3-Pro-Preview获得最高平均分（6.97/10），其次是Gemini-3-Flash-Preview（6.67）和Claude-4.5-Opus（6.46）；即使表现最佳的模型得分也低于最高分的70%。

Conclusion: 司法级的法律推理和文书写作对当前LLM仍然具有挑战性；释放完整的基准测试、模型输出和评估代码以支持法律AI能力的进一步研究。

Abstract: Existing benchmarks for legal AI focus primarily on tasks where LLMs must produce legal arguments or documents, yet the capacity to \emph{judge} such arguments -- weighing competing claims, applying doctrine to facts, and rendering reasoned decisions -- is arguably as fundamental to a well-functioning legal system as advocacy itself. We introduce Magis-Bench, a benchmark for evaluating LLMs on magistrate-level writing tasks derived from recent Brazilian competitive examinations for judicial positions. Magis-Bench comprises 74 questions from eight examinations conducted between 2023 and 2025, including discursive legal analysis questions with multi-turn structure and practical exercises requiring the composition of complete civil and criminal judicial sentences. We evaluate 23 state-of-the-art LLMs using an LLM-as-a-judge methodology with four independent frontier models as evaluators. Our results show strong inter-judge agreement (Kendall's $W = 0.984$; pairwise Kendall's $τ\ge 0.897$), with Google's Gemini-3-Pro-Preview achieving the highest average score (6.97/10), followed by Gemini-3-Flash-Preview (6.67) and Claude-4.5-Opus (6.46). Even the best-performing models score below 70\% of the maximum, indicating that judicial-level legal reasoning and writing remain challenging for current LLMs. We release the complete benchmark, model outputs, and evaluation code to support further research on legal AI capabilities.

</details>


### [54] [Can Language Models Identify Side Effects of Breast Cancer Radiation Treatments?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08439)
*Natalie Seah,Danielle S. Bitterman,Daphna Spiegel,Thomas Hartvigsen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究人员开发了一个面向部署的压力测试框架，用于评估大语言模型在生成乳腺癌治疗和生存护理中放疗副作用列表的可靠性，发现模型对微小文档变化敏感，且在召回罕见和长期副作用方面存在系统性不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确传达癌症治疗的副作用对癌症幸存者至关重要，特别是在知情同意等场景中。然而，由于临床知识缺陷和电子健康记录系统的碎片化，这一任务仍然具有挑战性。虽然大语言模型有潜力协助这项任务，但它们在肿瘤学生存护理背景下的可靠性仍不明确。

Method: 研究人员提出了一个部署导向的压力测试框架，使用21个乳腺癌患者档案构建配对的临床场景（仅在放疗方案上不同），评估了7个指令调优的大语言模型在多种提示策略下的表现。将模型输出与来自两家主要学术医疗中心知情同意文件的临床医生整理的参考标准进行比较，该参考标准将放疗剂量分割、照射野和位置与相关毒性关联起来，并按频率和时间发作进行分解。

Result: 研究发现：1）所有模型对微小文档变化都表现出敏感性；2）在精确率和召回率之间存在权衡；3）系统性地低估召回罕见和长期副作用；4）单独使用时，对生成副作用数量的限制会降低精确率；5）将输出基于临床医生整理的副作用列表可显著提高可靠性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 这些发现凸显了大语言模型在肿瘤学应用中的重要局限性，并提出了更安全、更具信息性的生存护理导向应用的实际设计选择，特别是需要将模型输出与临床医生整理的知识库相结合以提高可靠性。

Abstract: Accurately communicating the side effects of cancer treatments to cancer survivors is critical, particularly in settings such as informed consent, where clinicians must clearly and comprehensively convey potential treatment toxicities. However, this task remains challenging due to clinical knowledge deficits about adverse treatment effects and fragmentation across electronic health record (EHR) systems. Large language models (LLMs) have the potential to assist in this task, though their reliability in oncology survivorship contexts remains poorly understood. We present a deployment-oriented stress-testing framework for evaluating LLM-generated radiation side effect lists in breast cancer treatment and survivorship care. Using 21 breast cancer patient profiles, we construct paired patient clinical scenarios that differ only in radiotherapy regimens to evaluate seven instruction-tuned LLMs under multiple prompting regimes. We then compare LLM outputs to a clinician-curated reference derived from informed consent documents at two major academic medical centers and developed by a team including more than seven breast radiation oncologists. The reference maps radiation dose-fractionation, fields, and locations to associated toxicities, broken down by frequency and temporal onset. Across models, we reveal sensitivity to minor documentation changes, trade-offs between precision and recall, and systematic under-recall of rare and long-term side effects. When used alone, constraints on the number of side effects generated reduce precision, and grounding outputs in clinician-curated side effect lists substantially improves reliability and robustness. These findings highlight important limitations of LLM use in oncology and suggest practical design choices for safer and more informative survivorship-focused applications.

</details>


### [55] [Revisiting the syntax of imperatives in Yemeni Arabic: An Agree across phases approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08447)
*Mohammed Q. Shormani*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文重新审视也门阿拉伯语祈使句的句法结构，提出了一种"跨阶段一致"分析方法，成功解释了简单和复杂祈使句结构，包括A'-链结构，通过建立句法与语篇的紧密互动关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究对也门阿拉伯语祈使句的句法分析可能不够完善，特别是对于复杂祈使句结构和句法-语篇界面的处理。作者旨在提供一个更统一的分析框架，能够同时解释简单和复杂祈使句结构，并阐明句法与语篇信息结构之间的互动关系。

Method: 采用"跨阶段一致"理论框架，分析祈使句的句法结构。作者提出祈使句的题元主语是第二人称pro，而任何出现在动词前的显性代词或名词成分不是主语，而是C域的aboutness话题。这些话题作为祈使句的逻辑主语，并与pro建立共指关系。

Result: AAP方法成功解释了也门阿拉伯语中的简单和复杂祈使句结构，包括A'-链结构。研究表明句法-语篇界面的建立是由祈使句的解释性和表演性功能驱动的，将信息结构与命题结构联系起来。对于缺乏显性话题的核心祈使句，作者提出了一个空话题在Spec,TopP位置(重新)合并。

Conclusion: 也门阿拉伯语祈使句的句法分析应采用跨阶段一致方法，其中句法与语篇界面紧密互动，话题作为逻辑主语并与pro建立共指关系。这种关系分析为涉及匹配的APP操作，产生局部和非局部的A'-链。研究为祈使句的句法-语篇界面提供了新的理论见解。

Abstract: This article revisits the syntax of imperatives in Yemeni Arabic proposing an Agree acros phases (AAP) approach. I argue that the AAP approach successfully accounts for both simple and complex imperative constructions, including A'-chain structures, by establishing a close interactions between syntax and discourse. The study demonstrates that this interface is motivated by the interpretive and performative functions associated with imperatives, linking informational structure with propositional structure. It is also proposed that the thematic subject of imperatives is a 2-person pro, whereas any overt pronominal or nominal element occurring preverbally is not a subject, but rather a C-domain element, precisely aboutness topic. These topics serve as the logical subjects of imperatives and enter into a coreferentiality relationship with pro. This relation is analyzed as APP involving Match, yielding both local and non-local A'-chains. For core imperatives, viz., lacking an overt topic, I propose a null topic to (re)merge in Spec,TopP, whose interpretation depends on the discourse.

</details>


### [56] [PYTHALAB-MERA: Validation-Grounded Memory, Retrieval, and Acceptance Control for Frozen-LLM Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08468)
*Mehmet Iscan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PYTHALAB-MERA是一个轻量级的外部控制器，用于增强基于本地大语言模型的代码生成智能体。它通过验证导向的episodic memory、自适应检索-动作选择、延迟信用分配和结构化技能重用等功能，在强化学习编码任务中显著提升了验证通过率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有本地LLM编码智能体在需要执行反馈、持久状态和有界修复的环境中工作，但静态检索、长上下文提示、自我优化、执行反馈修复和基于权重的强化学习等方法不能共同提供验证导向的episodic memory、自适应检索-动作选择、延迟信用分配和结构化技能重用等功能。

Method: PYTHALAB-MERA作为一个轻量级外部控制器，围绕冻结的本地语言模型工作：1) LLM提出完整源文件；2) 控制器决定哪些记忆记录和AST衍生技能进入下一个提示；3) 通过快速失败管道验证每个候选方案；4) 将验证结果转换为有界成形奖励；5) 通过TD(λ)风格的资格迹传播延迟信用。

Result: 在严格的强化学习编码任务环境中，PYTHALAB-MERA在三个任务、三次重复、三次尝试预算的设置下，通过了8/9次严格验证；而自我优化基线和研究的GRACE扩展都通过了0/9次验证。

Conclusion: 结果表明，在这种记录的环境中，外部记忆和检索控制器提高了验证成功率。但这并不证明通用代码合成能力、最先进的性能、形式程序正确性或形式安全性。该研究支持一个有界的主张：在该特定设置下，外部控制器改进了验证成功。

Abstract: Local LLM-based coding agents increasingly work in settings where correctness is earned through execution feedback, persistent state, and bounded repair, not through a single fluent answer. Static retrieval, long-context prompting, self-refinement, execution-feedback repair, and reinforcement learning over model weights each address part of this setting, but they do not jointly provide validation-grounded episodic memory, adaptive retrieval-action selection, delayed credit assignment, and structural skill reuse around a frozen local model. We introduce PYTHALAB-MERA, a lightweight external controller for local validation-conditioned code generation. The frozen language model proposes complete source files; the controller decides which memory records and AST-derived skills should enter the next prompt, validates each candidate through a fail-fast pipeline, converts validation outcomes into bounded shaped rewards, and propagates delayed credit through TD(lambda)-style eligibility traces. We evaluate the implementation as a local CLI artifact on reinforcement-learning coding tasks with strict validation gates. In the measured hard RL setting with three tasks, three repetitions, and a three-attempt budget, PYTHALAB-MERA passed 8/9 strict validations; the self-refinement baseline and the investigated GRACE extension each passed 0/9. These results support a deliberately bounded claim: in this recorded setting, the external memory-and-retrieval controller improved validation success. They do not establish general-purpose code synthesis, state-of-the-art performance, formal program correctness, or formal safety.

</details>


### [57] [A Computational Operationalisation of Competing Maturational Theories of Syntactic Development via Statistical Grammar Induction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08476)
*Mila Marcheva,Suchir Salhan,Weiwei Sun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文通过计算建模比较两种句法发展阶段理论：GROWING理论（词类和屈折结构先出现）优于INWARD理论（话语相关范畴早期出现）


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究儿童第一语言发展中获得的中间句法范畴及其顺序，解决成熟理论的不同预测之间的争议

Method: 使用统计语法归纳计算化操作两种假设，在输入和学习算法相同的条件下，探索不同成熟顺序如何影响可学习的结构

Result: 基于计算操作化，GROWING理论在三个评估指标上显著优于INWARD理论

Conclusion: 计算建模支持儿童句法发展中词类和屈折结构先于话语相关范畴出现的理论，为语言发展机制提供了实证支持

Abstract: This paper is concerned with what intermediate syntactic categories children acquire during first language development, and in what order. Maturational theories make different predictions. Bottom-up accounts (GROWING) propose that lexical and inflectional structure emerges first, while inward accounts (INWARD) predict early access to discourse-related categories. We computationally operationalise these hypotheses of staged syntactic emergence using statistical grammar induction, asking what each proposed ordering makes learnable when input and learning algorithm are held constant. Our framework makes category acquisition explicit and allows us to explore how different maturational orderings shape the structure that can be learned under identical conditions. Based on this operationalisation, the GROWING account significantly outperforms the INWARD account across three evaluation metrics.

</details>


### [58] [Do Agents Need to Plan Step-by-Step? Rethinking Planning Horizon in Data-Centric Tool Calling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08477)
*Naoki Otani,Nikita Bhutani,Hannah Kim,Dan Zhang,Estevam Hruschka*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究表明，对于定义良好的数据中心任务，全视野规划（一次性生成完整计划）通过按需重规划可以实现与逐步规划（每一步交替推理）相同的准确度，同时使用2-3倍更少的token。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重新审视LLM智能体在复杂数据中心任务中的规划策略假设，特别是逐步执行的适应性必要性假设，探究规划视野对任务执行效率的影响。

Method: 采用对照实证研究，将规划视野作为关键架构特征隔离分析，系统研究拓扑复杂性和工具鲁棒性对两种规划范式（全视野规划和单步视野规划）的影响，在知识库问答和多跳问答任务上进行实验。

Result: 实验表明：全视野规划配合惰性重规划在不同深度、宽度和鲁棒性水平下都能达到与逐步规划相同的准确度，同时使用2-3倍更少的token。

Conclusion: 对于定义良好的数据中心任务，急切的逐步监控通常是不必要的，全视野规划配合按需重规划可以作为更高效的默认策略，平衡准确性和效率。

Abstract: Explicit planning is a critical capability for LLM-based agents solving complex data-centric tasks, which require precise tool calling over external data sources. Existing strategies fall into two paradigms based on planning horizon: (1) full-horizon (FH), which generates a complete plan before execution, and (2) single-step horizon (SH), which interleaves each action (tool call) with incremental reasoning and observation. While step-by-step execution is a common default under the assumption that eager execution monitoring is necessary for adaptability, we revisit this assumption for well-defined data-centric tasks. Our controlled empirical study isolates planning horizon as the key architectural feature and systematically analyzes the effects of topological complexity and tool robustness on both paradigms. Our experiments across Knowledge Base Question Answering and Multi-hop QA show that FH planning with lazy replanning achieves accuracy parity with SH across varying depths, breadths, and robustness levels, while using 2-3x fewer tokens. These findings suggest that for well-defined data-centric tasks, eager step-wise monitoring is often unnecessary, and full-horizon planning with on-demand replanning can offer a more efficient default.

</details>


### [59] [NARRA-Gym for Evaluating Interactive Narrative Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08503)
*Yue Huang,Yuchen Ma,Jiayi Ye,Wenjie Wang,Zipeng Ling,Xingjian Hu,Yuexing Hao,Zichen Chen,Zhangchen Xu,Yunhong He,Zhengqing Yuan,Yujun Zhou,Kehan Guo,Chaoran Chen,Toby Jia-Jun Li,Stefan Feuerriegel,Xiangliang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: NARRA-Gym是一个用于评估大语言模型在交互式叙事任务中表现的可执行环境，它通过从情感种子生成完整故事并记录完整轨迹，评估模型在故事生成、长上下文状态、角色模拟、共情个性化等多维度能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前交互式叙事任务的评估存在局限性：现有基准通常关注静态提示、孤立故事生成或事后评分，无法评估模型在故事生成、长上下文状态管理、角色模拟、共情个性化、故事相关产物生成等方面的综合能力，缺乏合适的基准来评估用户自适应的大语言模型行为。

Method: 研究者提出了NARRA-Gym评估环境，能够将稀疏的情感种子转化为完整的交互式故事片段，并记录模型在循环中的完整轨迹，包括故事构建、记忆更新、规划、节奏干预和可选的产物合成。使用受控的LLM-as-judge方法评估九个前沿大语言模型，涵盖八个基准角色，并进行人工评估，参与者对定制化模型输出进行评分。

Result: 结果显示模型表现存在显著差异：不同模型、角色和评估维度间变化很大。产生流畅故事的模型可能在鲁棒性、用户体验或抗阻力敏感性个性化方面表现不佳。这表明交互式叙事是一个有用的基准，可以评估超越孤立故事质量的长时程、用户自适应的大语言模型行为。

Conclusion: 交互式叙事任务提供了一个有价值的评估框架，能够全面检验大语言模型在长时程交互、用户适应性和多维度叙事能力方面的表现。NARRA-Gym环境能够揭示模型在不同维度的能力差异，对评估用户自适应的大语言模型行为具有重要意义。

Abstract: Interactive narrative tasks require LLMs to sustain a coherent, evolving story while adapting to a user over multiple turns. However, suitable benchmarks for this setting are limited: existing evaluations often focus on static prompts, isolated story generations, or post-hoc ratings, and therefore miss whether models can jointly manage story generation, long-context state and pacing, character simulation, empathic personalization, and story-grounded artifacts. We introduce NARRA-Gym, an executable evaluation environment that turns a sparse emotional seed into a complete interactive story episode and logs the full model-in-the-loop trajectory, including story construction, memory updates, planning, pacing interventions, and optional artifact synthesis. We evaluate nine frontier LLMs using a controlled LLM-as-judge sweep over eight benchmark personas and a human evaluation in which participants rate customized model outputs. Our results show substantial variation across models, personas, and evaluation dimensions: models that produce fluent stories can still fail on robustness, user experience, or resistance-sensitive personalization. These findings suggest that interactive narrative offers a useful benchmark for evaluating long-horizon, user-adaptive LLM behavior beyond isolated story quality.

</details>


### [60] [A Single Layer to Explain Them All:Understanding Massive Activations in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08504)
*Zeru Shi,Zhenting Wang,Fan Yang,Qifan Wang,Ruixiang Tang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文发现了大型语言模型中的"大规模激活层"(ME Layer)，提出了一种减少大规模激活令牌刚性的方法，以改善模型性能并缓解注意力汇聚问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究大规模激活在大型语言模型中的起源，特别是发现大规模激活会减少传递给注意力模块的隐藏表示多样性，限制了模型的表达能力。

Method: 首先识别出跨模型家族中一致的"大规模激活层"(ME Layer)，发现RMSNorm和FFN参数共同促成了大规模激活的形成。然后提出一种简单有效的方法来减少大规模激活令牌的刚性。

Result: 该方法在多种任务上（包括指令遵循和数学推理）都一致改善了LLM性能，在免训练和微调设置下均有效。同时证明了该方法能通过选择性地削弱注意力汇聚的影响来缓解它们。

Conclusion: 大规模激活层在LLMs中普遍存在，通过减少大规模激活令牌的刚性可以改善模型性能，这为理解注意力汇聚现象提供了新的视角，并揭示了基于隐藏状态层面的原则性缓解策略。
